   1++6D34              ; ============================================================================
   2++6D34              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6D34              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6D34              ;
   5++6D34              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6D34              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6D34              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6D34              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6D34              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6D34              ; ============================================================================
  11++6D34              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6D34              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6D34              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6D34              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6D34              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6D34              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6D34
  18++6D34              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6D34                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6D34 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6D37 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6D3A B7                   or      a
  23++6D3B ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6D3D C2 7C 6D             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6D40
  26++6D40                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6D40 21 24 91             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6D43 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6D46 B7                   or      a
  30++6D47 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6D49 DA 7C 6D             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6D4C CA 7C 6D             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6D4F 22 96 6D             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6D52 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6D53 FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6D55 D2 7C 6D             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6D58
  38++6D58 21 24 91             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6D5B 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6D5E B7                   or      a
  41++6D5F ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6D61 DA 6A 6D             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6D64 CA 6A 6D             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6D67 C3 7C 6D             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6D6A              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6D6A                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6D6A 3A 11 75             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6D6D FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6D6F C2 7C 6D             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6D72 3A 0E 75             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6D75 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6D77 C2 7C 6D             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6D7A B7                   or      a
  54++6D7B C9                   ret
  55++6D7C
  56++6D7C              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6D7C 37                   scf
  58++6D7D C9                   ret
  59++6D7E

  61++6D7E              p127_layout_report:

  70++6D96

 430+ 6DD0                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++6DD0              ; ============================================================================
   2++6DD0              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6DD0              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6DD0              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6DD0              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6DD0              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6DD0              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6DD0              ; ============================================================================
   9++6DD0              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6DD0              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6DD0              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6DD0
  13++6DD0              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6DD0              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6DD0              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6DD0              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6DD0              p127_resident_start:
  18++6DD0
  19++6DD0              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6DD0              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6DD0              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6DD0              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6DD0              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6DD0              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6DD0
  26++6DD0              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6DD0              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6DD0              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6DD0              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6DD0              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6DD0 32 5A 6E             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6DD3 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6DD4 32 5B 6E             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6DD7 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6DD8 32 5C 6E             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6DDB 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6DDC 32 5D 6E             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6DDF
  39++6DDF 3A 5A 6E             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6DE2 FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6DE4 C2 0E 6E             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6DE7 3A 5B 6E             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6DEA FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6DEC C2 0E 6E             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6DEF 3A 5C 6E             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6DF2 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6DF4 C2 0E 6E             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6DF7 3A 5D 6E             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6DFA E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6DFC CA 0E 6E             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6DFF                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6DFF 3A 5A 6E             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6E02 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6E04 CA 0E 6E             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6E07 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6E09 CA 0E 6E             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6E0C B7                   or      a
  58++6E0D C9                   ret
  59++6E0E
  60++6E0E              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6E0E 37                   scf
  62++6E0F C9                   ret
  63++6E10
  64++6E10              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6E10              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6E10              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++6E10 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++6E12 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++6E14 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++6E16 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++6E18 CD D0 6D             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++6E1B DA 58 6E             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++6E1E
  74++6E1E 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++6E20 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++6E22 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++6E24 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++6E26 CD D0 6D             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++6E29 D2 58 6E             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++6E2C
  81++6E2C 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++6E2E 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++6E30 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++6E32 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++6E34 CD D0 6D             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++6E37 D2 58 6E             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++6E3A

  90++6E3E 0E 02                ld      c,2

  93++6E45 D2 58 6E             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail

 349++7001              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++7001              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++7001              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++7001              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++7001              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++7001              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++7001              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.

   1++7050              ; ============================================================================
   2++7050              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++7050              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++7050              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++7050              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++7050              ; ============================================================================
   7++7050              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++7050              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++7050              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++7050              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++7050              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++7050              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++7050
  14++7050              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++7050              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++7050              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++7050              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++7050              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++7050              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++7050              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++7050              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++7050              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++7050              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++7050              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++7050
  26++7050              p127_irq_init:
  27++7050 F3                   di
  28++7051 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED

  31++7057 32 33 73             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++705A 32 34 73             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++705D 32 35 73             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++7060 B7                   or      a
  35++7061 C9                   ret

   1++7336              ; ============================================================================
   2++7336              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++7336              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4++7336              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++7336              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++7336              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++7336              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++7336              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++7336              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++7336              ; ============================================================================
  11++7336              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0

   1++754C              ; ============================================================================
   2++754C              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++754C              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++754C              ; ABI:
   5++754C              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++754C              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++754C              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++754C              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++754C              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++754C              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.
  11++754C              ; ============================================================================

   1++7666              ; ============================================================================
   2++7666              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++7666              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++7666              ;
   5++7666              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++7666              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++7666              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++7666              ;
   9++7666              ; API:
  10++7666              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++7666              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++7666              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++7666              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++7666              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++7666              ;
  16++7666              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++7666              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++7666              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++7666              ; ============================================================================
  20++7666              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++7666              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++7666              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++7666              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++7666              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++7666
  26++7666              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++7666              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++7666              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++7666              p127_arena_init:
  30++7666 21 4B 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  31++7669 0E 3F                ld      c,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  32++766B 06 40                ld      b,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  33++766D              .p127_arena_init_scan:
  34++766D 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  35++766E B7                   or      a
  36++766F C2 79 76             jp      nz,.p127_arena_init_guard_found
  37++7672 2B                   dec     hl
  38++7673 0D                   dec     c
  39++7674 10 F7                djnz    .p127_arena_init_scan
  40++7676 C3 2C 77             jp      p127_arena_reject
  41++7679              .p127_arena_init_guard_found:
  42++7679 0C                   inc     c
  43++767A 79                   ld      a,c
  44++767B FE 40                cp      P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  45++767D D2 2C 77             jp      nc,p127_arena_reject
  46++7680 32 BD 77             ld      (p127_arena_low_page),a
  47++7683 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  48++7685 91                   sub     c
  49++7686 CA 2C 77             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  50++7689 32 BE 77             ld      (p127_arena_page_count),a
  51++768C 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  52++768E 32 BF 77             ld      (p127_arena_next_page),a
  53++7691 B7                   or      a
  54++7692 C9                   ret
  55++7693
  56++7693              ; IN B=non-zero page count. OUT A=first page of the new block.
  57++7693              ; Validate the complete block before changing any page state.
  58++7693              p127_arena_alloc:
  59++7693 78                   ld      a,b
  60++7694 B7                   or      a
  61++7695 CA 2C 77             jp      z,p127_arena_reject
  62++7698 3A BF 77             ld      a,(p127_arena_next_page)
  63++769B 90                   sub     b
  64++769C DA 2C 77             jp      c,p127_arena_reject
  65++769F 32 C0 77             ld      (p127_arena_first_page),a
  66++76A2 4F                   ld      c,a
  67++76A3 3A BD 77             ld      a,(p127_arena_low_page)
  68++76A6 5F                   ld      e,a
  69++76A7 79                   ld      a,c
  70++76A8 93                   sub     e
  71++76A9 DA 2C 77             jp      c,p127_arena_reject
  72++76AC 48                   ld      c,b
  73++76AD 3A C0 77             ld      a,(p127_arena_first_page)
  74++76B0 5F                   ld      e,a
  75++76B1 16 00                ld      d,0
  76++76B3 21 0C 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
  77++76B6 19                   add     hl,de
  78++76B7              .p127_arena_alloc_check:

   1++77C4              ; ============================================================================
   2++77C4              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++77C4              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++77C4              ;
   5++77C4              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++77C4              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++77C4              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++77C4              ;
   9++77C4              ; API:
  10++77C4              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++77C4              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++77C4              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++77C4              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++77C4              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++77C4              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++77C4              ; ============================================================================
  17++77C4              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++77C4              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++77C4              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  20++77C4              P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT EQU 256
  21++77C4              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++77C4              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh
  23++77C4
  24++77C4              p127_as_init:
  25++77C4 21 65 79             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  26++77C7 36 FF                ld      (hl),P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  27++77C9 11 66 79             ld      de,p127_as_page_table+1

   1++7C45              ; ============================================================================
   2++7C45              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7C45              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7C45              ;
   5++7C45              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7C45              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7C45              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7C45              ;
   9++7C45              ; API inputs:
  10++7C45              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7C45              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7C45              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7C45              ; Outputs:
  14++7C45              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7C45              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7C45              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7C45              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7C45              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7C45              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7C45              ; ============================================================================
  21++7C45              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7C45              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7C45              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  24++7C45              P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE EQU 64
  25++7C45              P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE EQU 0C000h
  26++7C45

 177++82F3              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 178++82F3              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++82F3              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++82F3              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++82F3              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++82F3              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++82F3              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++82F3              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).
 185++82F3              p127_map_handoff_prepare:
 186++82F3                      ; Keep the target in E, invalidate any older staged request, then
 187++82F3                      ; validate the page/window and index the four-byte mask with DE=0:E.
 188++82F3 5F                   ld      e,a

 191++82F8 78                   ld      a,b

 312++83C1              ; Full four-window snapshot; commit changes RAM shadow state only.
 313++83C1              ; Rollback restores all four bytes, including after injected post-commit fault.
 314++83C1              ; No page-port OUT is performed by any routine in this block.

   1++84E9              ; ============================================================================
   2++84E9              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++84E9              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++84E9              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++84E9              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.
   6++84E9              ; ============================================================================
   7++84E9              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++84E9              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
   9++84E9              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++84E9
  11++84E9              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++84E9              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++84E9              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++84E9              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++84E9              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++84E9              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++84E9              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++84E9
  19++84E9              ; IN: none.
  20++84E9              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++84E9              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++84E9              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++84E9              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++84E9              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++84E9              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++84E9              p127_handoff_stack_probe:
  27++84E9 F3                   di
  28++84EA F5                   push    af
  29++84EB C5                   push    bc
  30++84EC D5                   push    de
  31++84ED E5                   push    hl
  32++84EE DD E5                push    ix
  33++84F0 FD E5                push    iy
  34++84F2 ED 73 81 85          ld      (p127_handoff_saved_sp),sp
  35++84F6
  36++84F6                      ; Refuse if the live stack has crossed its declared low-water limit.
  37++84F6 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
  38++84F9 39                   add     hl,sp
  39++84FA 11 00 B9             ld      de,P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP
  40++84FD B7                   or      a
  41++84FE ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++8500 DA 51 85             jp      c,p127_handoff_stack_refuse
  43++8503
  44++8503                      ; Canary bytes bracket the unused gap below the scratch stack.
  45++8503 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
  46++8505 32 FE B7             ld      (P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO),a
  47++8508 3E 5A                ld      a,05Ah
  48++850A 32 FF B7             ld      (P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI),a
  49++850D
  50++850D                      ; Switch only the software stack pointer. W2/P2 stays mapped.
  51++850D 31 80 B8             ld      sp,P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP
  52++8510 21 57 13             ld      hl,01357h
  53++8513 E5                   push    hl
  54++8514 11 68 24             ld      de,02468h
  55++8517 D5                   push    de
  56++8518 CD 69 85             call    p127_handoff_stack_inner
  57++851B DA 49 85             jp      c,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  58++851E D1                   pop     de
  59++851F 21 68 24             ld      hl,02468h
  60++8522 B7                   or      a
  61++8523 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  62++8525 C2 49 85             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  63++8528 E1                   pop     hl
  64++8529 11 57 13             ld      de,01357h
  65++852C B7                   or      a
  66++852D ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  67++852F C2 49 85             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  68++8532
  69++8532 3A FE B7             ld      a,(P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO)
  70++8535 FE A5                cp      0A5h
  71++8537 C2 49 85             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  72++853A 3A FF B7             ld      a,(P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI)
  73++853D FE 5A                cp      05Ah
  74++853F C2 49 85             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  75++8542

  77++8543 32 83 85             ld      (p127_handoff_result),a
  78++8546 C3 56 85             jp      p127_handoff_stack_restore
  79++8549
  80++8549              p127_handoff_stack_fail:
  81++8549 3E 01                ld      a,1
  82++854B 32 83 85             ld      (p127_handoff_result),a
  83++854E C3 56 85             jp      p127_handoff_stack_restore
  84++8551

  87++8553 32 83 85             ld      (p127_handoff_result),a
  88++8556
  89++8556              p127_handoff_stack_restore:
  90++8556                      ; Restore the exact saved SP before touching the caller's saved frame.
  91++8556 ED 7B 81 85          ld      sp,(p127_handoff_saved_sp)
  92++855A FD E1                pop     iy
  93++855C DD E1                pop     ix
  94++855E E1                   pop     hl
  95++855F D1                   pop     de

 100++8566 C8                   ret     z

 102++8568 C9                   ret

   1++8584              ; ============================================================================
   2++8584              ; Project127 TR-DOS directory reader
   3++8584              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++8584              ; Reads track 0 sectors 1..8 via the documented TR-DOS ROM entry #3D13.
   5++8584              ; Directory snapshot: 128 * 16 bytes at #C800 (reserved host/staging page P0).
   6++8584              ; Read-only: no file-data reads, writes, DMA, IM/EI or raw paging OUT.

  13++8584              ; The 240-byte usable interval #B700..#B7EF is below the handoff canary.
  14++8584              P127_TRDOS_SCRATCH_STACK_TOP EQU 0B7F0h

  17++8584              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_SIZE EQU 16

  20++8584              ; Output is mirrored by p127_puts/p127_put_char to the visible console and #EF.
  21++8584              ; Called immediately after the Project126-compatible legacy mapping
  22++8584              ; setup, before p127_ui_init changes the ROM/video/shadow-port state.

 442+ 8AD1                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_speaker.asm"
# file opened: p127_speaker.asm
   1++8AD1              ; ============================================================================
   2++8AD1              ; Project127 ATM Turbo 2+ beeper output driver
   3++8AD1              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8AD1              ;
   5++8AD1              ; Hardware: Spectrum/ATM #FE, D4 = speaker, D3 = tape output,
   6++8AD1              ; D0..D2 = border colour. OUT (#FE),A also presents A3 from A's bit 3
   7++8AD1              ; on ATM, so the caller-supplied D3 state is preserved.
   8++8AD1              ;
   9++8AD1              ; This is a low-level output latch only. It does not invent a timer,
  10++8AD1              ; enable interrupts, block the main loop, or emulate an IBM PC speaker.
  11++8AD1              ; The Z80 host owns this physical port. NeoGS must request sound through
  12++8AD1              ; the host transport/DMA and must never issue physical port I/O.
  13++8AD1              ;
  14++8AD1              ; API:
  15++8AD1              ;   p127_speaker_init  IN A = initial #FE low nibble (border + tape)
  16++8AD1              ;   p127_speaker_set   IN A bit 0 = speaker level (0/1)
  17++8AD1              ;   p127_speaker_toggle
  18++8AD1              ;   p127_speaker_stop
  19++8AD1              ;   p127_speaker_get   OUT A = shadow latch
  20++8AD1              ;
  21++8AD1              ; Caller must initialize with the actual current border/tape state before
  22++8AD1              ; using set/toggle/stop. No safe hardware readback of the #FE output latch
  23++8AD1              ; is assumed.
  24++8AD1              ; ============================================================================
  25++8AD1              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  26++8AD1              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  27++8AD1              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  28++8AD1
  29++8AD1              P127_SPEAKER_PORT EQU 0FEh
  30++8AD1              P127_SPEAKER_MASK EQU 010h
  31++8AD1              P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK EQU 00Fh
  32++8AD1
  33++8AD1              p127_speaker_init:
  34++8AD1 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  35++8AD3 32 2F 8B             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a

  38++8AD7              ; Set D4 while preserving border D0..D2 and tape D3.
  39++8AD7              ; OUT (#FE),A intentionally uses A3 as part of the ATM border/BRIGHT
  40++8AD7              ; address decode; preserving D3 also preserves that address-line state.
  41++8AD7              p127_speaker_set:

  58++8AEF C1                   pop     bc
  59++8AF0 C9                   ret
  60++8AF1
  61++8AF1              ; Invert only D4. Border/tape state remains unchanged.
  62++8AF1              p127_speaker_toggle:

  66++8AF9 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  67++8AFB C9                   ret
  68++8AFC
  69++8AFC              ; Force speaker low without changing D0..D3.

  74++8B04 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  75++8B06 C9                   ret

  83++8B0B              ; 3.5 MHz; pitch therefore depends on the active ATM CPU clock. This is
  84++8B0B              ; intentionally a one-shot diagnostic, not the asynchronous sound service.

   2++8E06              ; Project127 FAT16 root-directory reader
   3++8E06              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8E06              ; Derived from Project127/113 FAT16 BPB/root-directory flow.
   5++8E06              ; Uses the existing NEMO-DIVIDE LBA28 single-sector transport in p127_hdd.asm.
   6++8E06              ; Read-only: no writes, DMA, IRQ, IM/EI or paging OUT.
   7++8E06              ; Output: each root entry is mirrored to the ATM console and debug port #EF.
   8++8E06              ; ============================================================================

  14++8E06              ; read every root-directory sector and print each valid 8.3 entry.

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret

 129+ A081
 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly
 133+ A081              ; as in Project126's atm_host implementation.
 134+ A081              p126_atm_restore_dosen:

  93  A40C                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_physmem.asm"
# file opened: p127_physmem.asm
   1+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A40C              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A40C              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A40C              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A40C              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A40C              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A40C              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A40C
  15+ A40C              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A40C              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A40C              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A40C              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A40C              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A40C              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A40C              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:
  22+ A40C 3E 53                ld      a,'S'
  23+ A40E CD BA AA             call    p127_physmem_trace
  24+ A411 AF                   xor     a
  25+ A412 32 7D AB             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
  26+ A415 32 7E AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  27+ A418 32 7F AB             ld      (p127_physmem_quiesced),a
  28+ A41B 32 80 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  29+ A41E 32 81 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a
  30+ A421 32 82 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  31+ A424
  32+ A424                      ; The host/staging page must be the documented W3 page before entry.
  33+ A424 3A C0 83             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
  34+ A427 B7                   or      a
  35+ A428 C2 BF A6             jp      nz,p127_physmem_fail_map_state
  36+ A42B
  37+ A42B CD B3 73             call    p127_mem_alloc
  38+ A42E DA C4 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_alloc
  39+ A431 32 83 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_a),a
  40+ A434 32 8B AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_list),a
  41+ A437 3E 01                ld      a,1
  42+ A439 32 7E AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  43+ A43C
  44+ A43C CD B3 73             call    p127_mem_alloc
  45+ A43F DA C4 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_alloc
  46+ A442 32 84 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_b),a
  47+ A445 32 8C AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_list+1),a
  48+ A448 3E 02                ld      a,2
  49+ A44A 32 7E AB             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  50+ A44D
  51+ A44D 3A 83 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
  52+ A450 47                   ld      b,a
  53+ A451 3A 84 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_b)
  54+ A454 B8                   cp      b
  55+ A455 CA C4 A6             jp      z,p127_physmem_fail_alloc
  56+ A458
  57+ A458                      ; No UI routine may run while W3 is reassigned.
  58+ A458 CD 8E 6E             call    p127_w3_quiesce
  59+ A45B DA C9 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_lease
  60+ A45E 3E 01                ld      a,1
  61+ A460 32 7F AB             ld      (p127_physmem_quiesced),a
  62+ A463 CD AD 6E             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
  63+ A466 DA C9 A6             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_lease
  64+ A469 3E 01                ld      a,1
  65+ A46B 32 80 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  66+ A46E
  67+ A46E                      ; Preserve one byte from P0 for a non-destructive port-open probe.
  68+ A46E 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
  69+ A471 32 86 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_p0),a
  70+ A474 2F                   cpl

 420+ A750              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:

 442+ A77D                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A77D                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A77D                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.

 448+ A782              p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error:
 449+ A782 37                   scf
 450+ A783 C9                   ret
 451+ A784
 452+ A784              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A784              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A784              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A784                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A784                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A784                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A784                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.
 459+ A784 CD 11 A0             call    p126_atm_video_init
 460+ A787
 461+ A787                      ; Fixed-row probe: if this label is visible, the selected alternate
 462+ A787                      ; text bank (P7 characters / P3 attributes) and PrintAt path agree.
 463+ A787 21 2E A8             ld      hl,p127_physmem_screen_video_line
 464+ A78A 3E 04                ld      a,4
 465+ A78C 0E 01                ld      c,1

 491+ A7C6 5F                   ld      e,a

 525+ A805              ; HL=zero-terminated string. The console routine preserves HL.
 526+ A805              p127_physmem_screen_puts:

 529+ A807 C8                   ret     z

 532+ A80A CD D8 A1             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
 533+ A80D E1                   pop     hl

 536+ A810              ; A=page ID. Emit two hexadecimal digits through the visible UI console.

 570+ A8B9              p127_physmem_cleanup_error:

 573+ A8BE 37                   scf
 574+ A8BF C9                   ret
 575+ A8C0
 576+ A8C0              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ A8C0              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ A8C0              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ A8C0 21 0C 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ A8C3 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ A8C5 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ A8C7              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ A8C7 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ A8C8 FE 06                cp      6
 585+ A8CA 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ A8CC FE 07                cp      7
 587+ A8CE 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ A8D0 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ A8D2 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ A8D4 FE 01                cp      1
 591+ A8D6 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ A8D8 FE 02                cp      2
 593+ A8DA 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ A8DC FE 03                cp      3
 595+ A8DE 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ A8E0 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ A8E2 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ A8E4 FE 05                cp      5
 599+ A8E6 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ A8E8 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ A8EA              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ A8EA C5                   push    bc
 603+ A8EB E5                   push    hl
 604+ A8EC 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ A8ED 16 00                ld      d,0
 606+ A8EF 21 0C 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 607+ A8F2 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ A8F3 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 609+ A8F4 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 610+ A8F6 E1                   pop     hl
 611+ A8F7 C1                   pop     bc
 612+ A8F8 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ A8FA              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:
 614+ A8FA 0C                   inc     c
 615+ A8FB 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ A8FD B7                   or      a
 617+ A8FE C9                   ret
 618+ A8FF              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ A8FF 37                   scf
 620+ A900 C9                   ret
 621+ A901
 622+ A901              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ A901              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ A901 3A 7E AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ A904 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ A905 21 8B AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ A908              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:
 628+ A908 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 629+ A909 5F                   ld      e,a
 630+ A90A 16 00                ld      d,0
 631+ A90C C5                   push    bc
 632+ A90D E5                   push    hl
 633+ A90E 21 0C 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 634+ A911 19                   add     hl,de
 635+ A912 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 636+ A913 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 637+ A915 E1                   pop     hl
 638+ A916 C1                   pop     bc
 639+ A917 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail
 640+ A919 23                   inc     hl
 641+ A91A 10 EC                djnz    p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop
 642+ A91C B7                   or      a
 643+ A91D C9                   ret
 644+ A91E              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ A91E 37                   scf
 646+ A91F C9                   ret
 647+ A920
 648+ A920              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ A920              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ A920              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ A920 21 0C 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ A923 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ A925 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ A927              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ A927 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ A928 FE 00                cp      0
 657+ A92A 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ A92C FE 01                cp      1
 659+ A92E 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ A930 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ A932 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ A934 FE 03                cp      3
 663+ A936 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ A938 FE 04                cp      4
 665+ A93A 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ A93C FE 05                cp      5
 667+ A93E 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ A940 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ A942 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ A944 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ A945 B7                   or      a
 672+ A946 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ A948 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ A94A              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ A94A 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ A94B FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ A94D 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ A94F              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ A94F 23                   inc     hl
 680+ A950 0C                   inc     c
 681+ A951 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ A953 B7                   or      a
 683+ A954 C9                   ret
 684+ A955              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ A955 37                   scf
 686+ A956 C9                   ret
 687+ A957
 688+ A957              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ A957              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ A957
 691+ A957              ; ============================================================================
 692+ A957              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ A957              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ A957              ; Public ABI:
 695+ A957              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ A957              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ A957              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ A957              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ A957              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ A957              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ A957              ; ============================================================================
 702+ A957              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ A957 C3 B3 73             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ A95A
 705+ A95A              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ A95A 32 B7 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ A95D 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ A960 B7                   or      a
 709+ A961 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ A964 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ A967 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ A968 3A C0 83             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ A96B B8                   cp      b
 714+ A96C CA 14 AA             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ A96F 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ A972 C3 C9 73             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ A975
 718+ A975              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ A975              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ A975 F3                   di
 721+ A976 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ A979 B7                   or      a
 723+ A97A C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ A97D 3A C0 83             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ A980 B7                   or      a
 726+ A981 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ A984 CD 8E 6E             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ A987 D8                   ret     c
 729+ A988 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ A98A 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ A98D CD AD 6E             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ A990 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ A992 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ A994 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ A997 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ A99A 32 B6 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ A99D 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ A99F 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ A9A2 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ A9A5 B7                   or      a
 741+ A9A6 C9                   ret
 742+ A9A7              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ A9A7 CD D5 6E             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ A9AA AF                   xor     a
 745+ A9AB 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ A9AE 37                   scf
 747+ A9AF C9                   ret
 748+ A9B0
 749+ A9B0              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ A9B0              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ A9B0 32 B7 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ A9B3 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ A9B6 FE 01                cp      1
 754+ A9B8 C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ A9BB 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ A9BE FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ A9C0 D2 14 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ A9C3 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ A9C4 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ A9C6 21 0C 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ A9C9 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ A9CA 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ A9CB FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ A9CD C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ A9D0 3A B7 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ A9D3 C3 40 AB             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ A9D6
 768+ A9D6              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ A9D6              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ A9D6              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ A9D6 3A B2 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ A9D9 FE 01                cp      1
 773+ A9DB C2 14 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ A9DE AF                   xor     a
 775+ A9DF CD 40 AB             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ A9E2 D8                   ret     c
 777+ A9E3 3A B5 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ A9E6 B7                   or      a
 779+ A9E7 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ A9E9 3A B6 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ A9EC 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ A9EF              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ A9EF 3A B4 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ A9F2 B7                   or      a
 785+ A9F3 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ A9F5 CD C1 6E             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ A9F8 D8                   ret     c
 788+ A9F9 AF                   xor     a
 789+ A9FA 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ A9FD              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ A9FD 3A B3 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ AA00 B7                   or      a
 793+ AA01 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ AA03 CD D5 6E             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ AA06 D8                   ret     c
 796+ AA07 AF                   xor     a
 797+ AA08 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ AA0B              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ AA0B AF                   xor     a
 800+ AA0C 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ AA0F 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ AA12 B7                   or      a
 803+ AA13 C9                   ret
 804+ AA14
 805+ AA14              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ AA14 37                   scf
 807+ AA15 C9                   ret
 808+ AA16
 809+ AA16              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ AA16              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ AA16              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ AA16 AF                   xor     a
 813+ AA17 32 B2 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ AA1A 32 B3 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ AA1D 32 B4 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ AA20 32 B5 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ AA23 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ AA25 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ AA28 D2 B0 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ AA2B CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AA2E DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AA31 32 B8 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AA34 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AA37 32 B9 AA             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AA3A CD 75 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AA3D DA AA AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AA40                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AA40 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AA42 CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AA45 D2 A7 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AA48 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AA4B CD B0 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AA4E DA A7 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AA51                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AA51 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AA54 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AA57 D2 A7 AA             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AA5A 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AA5C 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AA5F CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AA62 DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AA65 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AA68 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AA69 3A B9 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AA6C B8                   cp      b
 846+ AA6D C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AA70 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AA73 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AA76 DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AA79                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AA79 CD 57 A9             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AA7C DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AA7F 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AA80 3A B8 AA             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AA83 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AA84 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AA87 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AA88 CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AA8B DA B0 AA             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AA8E 3A FB 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AA91 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AA93 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AA96 3A FC 6F             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AA99 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AA9B C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AA9E 3A C0 83             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AAA1 B7                   or      a
 868+ AAA2 C2 B0 AA             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AAA5 B7                   or      a
 870+ AAA6 C9                   ret
 871+ AAA7
 872+ AAA7              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AAA7 CD D6 A9             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AAAA              p127_physmem_api_test_free:

 876+ AAAD CD 5A A9             call    p127_physmem_free_page

 890+ AABA              p127_physmem_trace:
 891+ AABA 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 892+ AABD ED 79                out     (c),a
 893+ AABF C9                   ret
 894+ AAC0
 895+ AAC0              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AAC0              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AAC0              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AAC0              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AAC0 F5                   push    af
 900+ AAC1 C5                   push    bc
 901+ AAC2 D5                   push    de
 902+ AAC3 E5                   push    hl
 903+ AAC4 FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ AAC6 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify
 905+ AAC8 21 12 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_fill_text
 906+ AACB 18 03                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 907+ AACD              p127_physmem_trace_page_verify:
 908+ AACD 21 20 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_verify_text
 909+ AAD0              p127_physmem_trace_page_text:
 910+ AAD0 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 911+ AAD1 B7                   or      a
 912+ AAD2 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_trace_page_value
 913+ AAD4 23                   inc     hl
 914+ AAD5 E5                   push    hl
 915+ AAD6 CD 0C AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 916+ AAD9 E1                   pop     hl
 917+ AADA 18 F4                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 918+ AADC              p127_physmem_trace_page_value:
 919+ AADC 3A 89 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 920+ AADF CD F1 AA             call    p127_physmem_trace_hex8
 921+ AAE2 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 922+ AAE4 CD 0C AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 923+ AAE7 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 924+ AAE9 CD 0C AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 925+ AAEC E1                   pop     hl
 926+ AAED D1                   pop     de
 927+ AAEE C1                   pop     bc
 928+ AAEF F1                   pop     af
 929+ AAF0 C9                   ret
 930+ AAF1
 931+ AAF1              p127_physmem_trace_hex8:
 932+ AAF1 F5                   push    af
 933+ AAF2 E6 F0                and     0F0h
 934+ AAF4 0F                   rrca
 935+ AAF5 0F                   rrca
 936+ AAF6 0F                   rrca
 937+ AAF7 0F                   rrca
 938+ AAF8 CD 01 AB             call    p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 939+ AAFB F1                   pop     af
 940+ AAFC E6 0F                and     00Fh
 941+ AAFE C3 01 AB             jp      p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 942+ AB01
 943+ AB01              p127_physmem_trace_nibble:
 944+ AB01 5F                   ld      e,a
 945+ AB02 16 00                ld      d,0
 946+ AB04 21 30 AB             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_hex_table
 947+ AB07 19                   add     hl,de
 948+ AB08 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 949+ AB09 C3 0C AB             jp      p127_physmem_trace_char
 950+ AB0C
 951+ AB0C              p127_physmem_trace_char:
 952+ AB0C 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 953+ AB0F ED 79                out     (c),a
 954+ AB11 C9                   ret
 955+ AB12
 956+ AB12              p127_physmem_trace_fill_text:
 957+ AB12 46 49 4C 4C          db      "FILL PAGE ID=",0
 957+ AB16 20 50 41 47
 957+ AB1A 45 20 49 44
 957+ AB1E 3D 00

 959+ AB20 56 45 52 49          db      "VERIFY PAGE ID=",0
 959+ AB24 46 59 20 50
 959+ AB28 41 47 45 20
 959+ AB2C 49 44 3D 00

 961+ AB34 34 35 36 37
 961+ AB38 38 39 41 42
 961+ AB3C 43 44 45 46
 962+ AB40
 963+ AB40              ; IN A=physical RAM page ID. Program only W3 (#FFF7), never W0/W1/W2.
 964+ AB40              ; D6=1 selects RAM, D7=0 selects the inverse D0-D5 page ID.
 965+ AB40              p127_physmem_set_w3:
 966+ AB40 32 85 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_page_arg),a
 967+ AB43 CD DD 81             call    p127_map_encode_page
 968+ AB46 D8                   ret     c
 969+ AB47 01 F7 FF             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT
 970+ AB4A ED 79                out     (c),a
 971+ AB4C 3A 85 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_arg)
 972+ AB4F 32 C0 83             ld      (p127_map_shadow+3),a
 973+ AB52 B7                   or      a
 974+ AB53 C9                   ret
 975+ AB54
 976+ AB54              ; IN D=pattern seed. Byte(address)=ROL(address_low) XOR address_high XOR D.
 977+ AB54              ; The same address produces a different expected value at each seed.
 978+ AB54              p127_physmem_fill_pattern:
 979+ AB54 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 980+ AB57 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 981+ AB5A              .p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop:
 982+ AB5A 7D                   ld      a,l
 983+ AB5B 07                   rlca
 984+ AB5C AC                   xor     h
 985+ AB5D AA                   xor     d
 986+ AB5E 77                   ld      (hl),a
 987+ AB5F 23                   inc     hl
 988+ AB60 0B                   dec     bc
 989+ AB61 78                   ld      a,b
 990+ AB62 B1                   or      c
 991+ AB63 20 F5                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop
 992+ AB65 C9                   ret
 993+ AB66
 994+ AB66              ; IN D=pattern seed. Verify all 16 KiB against the address-derived pattern.
 995+ AB66              p127_physmem_verify_pattern:
 996+ AB66 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 997+ AB69 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 998+ AB6C              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop:
 999+ AB6C 7D                   ld      a,l
1000+ AB6D 07                   rlca
1001+ AB6E AC                   xor     h
1002+ AB6F AA                   xor     d
1003+ AB70 BE                   cp      (hl)
1004+ AB71 20 08                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail
1005+ AB73 23                   inc     hl
1006+ AB74 0B                   dec     bc
1007+ AB75 78                   ld      a,b
1008+ AB76 B1                   or      c
1009+ AB77 20 F3                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop
1010+ AB79 B7                   or      a
1011+ AB7A C9                   ret
1012+ AB7B              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail:
1013+ AB7B 37                   scf
1014+ AB7C C9                   ret
